- **problem-frames**: Michael Jackson Problem Frames 方法論：在寫 spec 之前，把問題拆成 R（需求）/ S（規格）/ W（領域假設），並證明 S ∧ W ⟹ R，藉此把領域假設前置顯式化。 適用情境：用戶提到「problem frame」「問題框架」「領域假設」「規格邊界」「機器與世界」 「R/S/W」「需求 vs 規格」「正確性論證」「frame concern」「需求太模糊先框問題」時觸發。 也適用於：需求把「機器要做什麼」與「世界要成立什麼」混在一起、 或 AI 老是自行補不同假設導致規格不可重現時。 位於 event-storming 之後、spectra-amplifier 之前，產出 problem-frame.md 作為展開規格的前置輸入。 ([heyu-ai/yibi-stack](https://github.com/heyu-ai/yibi-stack/tree/HEAD/plugins/methodology/skills/problem-frames) · ★1)

Activates on "diagnose this problem", "root cause", "why isn't this working", "what's really going on", "problem framing", "five whys", or "systems thinking." ([hollandkevint/thinkhaven-method-kit](https://github.com/hollandkevint/thinkhaven-method-kit/tree/HEAD/skills/problem-framing))
- **problem-framing**: Turn an idea, feature request, customer complaint or founder hunch into a clear problem brief — who has the problem, the job and circumstance, evidence, current alternatives, why now, the outcome it should move, the four product risks and the riskiest assumption to test, and an appetite. Use for "we should build X", "customers keep asking for Y", "is this worth doing?", or before any spec. ([HPAIF/ai-product](https://github.com/HPAIF/ai-product/tree/HEAD/skills/problem-framing))
- **problem-framing**: Use when a product problem is unclear, a proposed solution needs reframing, or affected users, evidence, and desired outcomes are not yet agreed. ([j4ch3n/Battuta](https://github.com/j4ch3n/Battuta/tree/HEAD/skills-hub/problem-framing))
- **problem-framing**: Frame a proposed feature, defect, workflow, or system change before implementation by identifying users, outcomes, evidence, constraints, unknowns, options, and measurable success. Use when a request is important but the problem or desired result is not yet decision-ready. ([JovaniPink/skills](https://github.com/JovaniPink/skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/jovanipink-engineering/skills/problem-framing) · 3 copies)
- **problem-framing**: Turns unowned, cross-cutting ambiguity into a crisp, falsifiable problem statement before any solutioning. Use when a problem spans teams with no owner, when stakeholders disagree on what the problem even is, when a request arrives pre-shaped as a solution, or when work keeps stalling because its goal is contested. ([Kart-rc/nlah-agent-repo](https://github.com/Kart-rc/nlah-agent-repo/tree/HEAD/harness/skillpacks/distinguished-engineer/problem-framing))
- **problem-framing**: Frames the problem behind a feature request or a vague goal - who is stuck, where, what evidence exists, how success would be observed, and what is out of scope - as a draft with its assumptions marked. Use when the user asks to frame a problem, write a problem statement, or work out what problem a requested feature solves before building it. Not for brainstorming options or writing a finished spec. ([modootoday/marketplace](https://github.com/modootoday/marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/product-planning/skills/problem-framing))
- **problem-framing**: Frame the problem before any solution — symptom vs root need, who/what/why, constraints, premature-solution bias. A problem statement, not options (brainstorm) or root-cause (5-whys). ([MohammadBafkar/polymath](https://github.com/MohammadBafkar/polymath/tree/HEAD/plugins/polymath-thinking/skills/problem-framing))
- **problem-framing**: Turn a vague request, goal, or proposed solution into a precise, neutral, evidence-based problem statement before any solution work begins. Use this whenever someone describes what they want to build or change but has not clearly stated the underlying problem — phrases like "we need a dashboard", "can we add X", "users are unhappy", "engagement is low", "let's build Y", or any goal stated without a grounded problem. This is the foundational step of discovery; run it first. It produces a `problem-statement.md` artifact and actively challenges solution-first thinking. ([omprakash-9939/Travel](https://github.com/omprakash-9939/Travel/tree/HEAD/business-discovery-skills/business-discovery-skills/problem-framing))
- **problem-framing**: Turns a vague, shifting or solution-shaped request into a short written problem brief with an explicit goal, in-scope and out-of-scope lists, constraints, testable acceptance criteria, an agreed glossary and marked open questions, so the work that follows is narrow and checkable instead of plausible and wrong. Detects the XY problem (asking about the attempted solution), target drift (starts as A, turns out to be B) and circumlocution (describing a thing by its function because the term is missing), and resolves a missing term by elicitation instead of silently paraphrasing it into confident… ([OneLiteFeatherNET/onelitefeather-claude-marketplace](https://github.com/OneLiteFeatherNET/onelitefeather-claude-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/problem-framing/skills/problem-framing))
- **problem-framing**: Find who has the problem, what it costs and what solved looks like, before any solution. Use when an ask arrives as a solution ("we need a dashboard", "we need an agent") or a client request is fuzzy. Not for generating options (use idea-refine) or reviewing a written draft (use pm-review). ([pratapme1/Vi_Framework](https://github.com/pratapme1/Vi_Framework/tree/HEAD/skills/problem-framing))
- **problem-framing**: Write a problem statement before any solution is chosen, covering who has the problem, the evidence, what they do today, why now and what is unknown, as a Confluence draft the PM approves. Use when the PM has a feature request, a customer ask or a hunch and needs to pin down the user and the problem first, or says "frame this", "what problem are we solving" or "write the problem statement". The draft contains no solution. Not for requirements (use prd-draft) or for choosing between options (use decision-doc). ([shariff25/PM-Operating-System](https://github.com/shariff25/PM-Operating-System/tree/HEAD/content/skills/problem-framing))
- **problem-framing**: Мета-фреймворк структурированного решения любых задач. Определяет область знаний, находит существующие фреймворки через web research, валидирует подходы, выявляет типичные ошибки и constraints. ОБЯЗАТЕЛЬНО использует sequential thinking и web research на каждом запуске. Активировать только при явном запросе пользователя на "problem-framing", "фрейминг задачи" или "структурированный анализ задачи". ([shivamchopra7/claude-skill-directory](https://github.com/shivamchopra7/claude-skill-directory/tree/HEAD/skills/analysis/problem-framing-merllinsbeard-ai-claude-skills-col-2) · 2 copies)
- **problem-framing**: Full rubric for framing a request before implementing it — separating Goal, Observation, Hypothesis and Verification, and choosing between READY, CLARIFY, INVESTIGATE and PROCEED_WITH_ASSUMPTIONS. Use when a request's premise is unverified, its goal or success criterion is unclear, or an asserted cause ("X is the bottleneck", "it's a caching issue") would otherwise drive a code change. Do not use for requests that are already clear or trivial. ([togishima/premise-plugin](https://github.com/togishima/premise-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/problem-framing))
- **problem-framing**: Enforces disciplined problem discovery before any solution — digging to the ROOT of a user problem with the 5 Whys, breaking it into aspects, and writing a formal problem statement (root + aspect + who + when + why) plus a problem checklist (audience, goal, needs, consequences to avoid) that converts into a user story. Use when framing a problem, writing or reviewing a problem statement or problem checklist, running discovery, finding the root cause of a user pain, defining who/when/why a problem matters, or turning a vague complaint into a validated problem — even if the user doesn't say "sk… ([ValeriyaMetla/AI-Development-Residency](https://github.com/ValeriyaMetla/AI-Development-Residency/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/problem-framing))
- **problem-framing**: 本来の問題は何か、なぜ解く価値があるかを検討するときに使う。 ([watanabetatsumi/my-product-harness](https://github.com/watanabetatsumi/my-product-harness/tree/HEAD/skills/problem-framing))

Not for a single persona-centred problem statement (use problem-statement). After the frame is settled use prd for the spec; for a quick code-facing problem and constraints pass before implementation use spec-the-problem. - **procedimiento**: Procedimiento administrativo — guía a través del procedimiento administrativo común. Cubre plazos (art. 21 LPAC), silencio administrativo (arts. 24-25), recursos administrativos (arts. 112-126) con árbol de decisión para alzada, reposición y revisión extraordinaria. Usar cuando el usuario diga "me han notificado una resolución", "recurso administrativo", "silencio", "plazo para recurrir", "procedimiento administrativo". ([ThomasMoreAI/legal-skills-open](https://github.com/ThomasMoreAI/legal-skills-open/tree/HEAD/es/administrative/skills/procedimiento) · ★136 · 2 copies)
- **procedimiento**: Triaje de procedimiento tributario — analiza la situación procesal tributaria del usuario e identifica el tipo de procedimiento, plazos, opciones y estrategia. Cubre: comprobación limitada, inspección, sancionador, reclamación ante TEA y recurso contencioso-administrativo. Usar cuando el usuario diga "me ha llegado una notificación de Hacienda", "inspección", "requerimiento", "sanción tributaria", "recurso TEA". ([betobetico/claude-para-abogados](https://github.com/betobetico/claude-para-abogados/tree/HEAD/fiscal/skills/procedimiento) · ★49)
- **procedimiento**: Triaje de procedimiento tributario — analiza la situación procesal tributaria del usuario e identifica el tipo de procedimiento, plazos, opciones y estrategia. Cubre: requerimiento de información, fiscalización /auditoría, liquidación adicional, sancionador, recurso de reconsideración, apelación ante el Tribunal Administrativo Tributario (TAT) y demanda contencioso-administrativa ante la Sala Tercera de la CSJ. Usar cuando el usuario diga "me ha llegado una notificación de la DGI", "fiscalización", "requerimiento", "multa tributaria", "recurso ante el TAT". ([betobetico/claude-para-abogados-panama](https://github.com/betobetico/claude-para-abogados-panama/tree/HEAD/fiscal/skills/procedimiento))
- **procedimiento**: Procedimiento administrativo — guía a través del procedimiento administrativo general de la Ley 38 de 2000. Cubre plazos, silencio administrativo y recursos administrativos (reconsideración y apelación) con árbol de decisión para agotar la vía gubernativa. Usar cuando el usuario diga "me han notificado una resolución", "recurso administrativo", "silencio", "plazo para recurrir", "procedimiento administrativo". ([betobetico/claude-para-abogados-panama](https://github.com/betobetico/claude-para-abogados-panama/tree/HEAD/administrativo/skills/procedimiento))
- **procedimiento-abreviado-ca**: Guía y redacción del procedimiento abreviado contencioso-administrativo en sede JUDICIAL (art. 78 LJCA, redacción LO 1/2025) — ámbito por materia y por cuantía, demanda inicial directa, vista rogada y motivada, expediente electrónico y sentencia oral. Activar con "procedimiento abreviado contencioso", "art. 78 LJCA", "demanda abreviado", "llevar la multa ya firme en vía administrativa al juzgado", "recurso de personal", "extranjería", "asunto de menos de 30.000 euros". Requisito previo: vía administrativa AGOTADA. Si la sanción está todavía en tramitación administrativa (pliego de cargos, pro… ([DerechoVirtual/jurisprudenciator](https://github.com/DerechoVirtual/jurisprudenciator/tree/HEAD/plugins/litigacion-contencioso-espana/skills/procedimiento-abreviado-ca) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **procedimiento-abreviado-defensa-conforme-acusacion-salvo-697**: Redacta el escrito «Procedimiento abreviado: defensa conforme con la acusación salvo en la responsabilidad civil» (formulario 697 de instrucción y procedimiento penal, revisado el 17-11-2026): pide los datos, decide las opciones, aplica las correcciones y comprueba las normas en el BOE. - **procedimiento-sancionador-ca**: Catálogo (sin plantilla). Defensa en la fase ADMINISTRATIVA del procedimiento sancionador (arts. 25-31 Ley 40/2015 y arts. 53, 64, 77, 85, 89-90, 95-96 LPAC), antes de llegar al juzgado. Cubre caducidad, prescripción de infracciones y sanciones, atipicidad, culpabilidad, presunción de veracidad de las actas, defectos de notificación, pago voluntario con reducción y el recorrido alzada/reposición → contencioso. Activar con "expediente sancionador", "pliego de cargos", "alegaciones a la denuncia", "propuesta de resolución", "caducidad del expediente", "prescripción de la infracción", "boletín d… ([DerechoVirtual/jurisprudenciator](https://github.com/DerechoVirtual/jurisprudenciator/tree/HEAD/plugins/litigacion-contencioso-espana/skills/procedimiento-sancionador-ca) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **procedimientos-y-formacion-hotel**: Redacta procedimientos operativos (SOP) por departamento de hotel y los convierte en material de formación para personal nuevo. Úsala cuando haya que documentar cómo se hace una tarea o formar a alguien que acaba de entrar. ([Nawii99/arsenal-piezas](https://github.com/Nawii99/arsenal-piezas/tree/HEAD/plugins/negocio-varios/skills/procedimientos-y-formacion-hotel))
- **procedura-aml**: Buduje z polską instytucją obowiązaną (biuro rachunkowe, pośrednik nieruchomości, biuro wirtualne, kantor i inne) jej dokumentację AML, czyli ocenę ryzyka instytucji, procedurę wewnętrzną, ocenę ryzyka klienta z punktacją liczoną skryptem ze śladem obliczeń, ewidencje i checklisty środków bezpieczeństwa finansowego. Używaj przy hasłach AML, pranie pieniędzy, GIIF, instytucja obowiązana, beneficjent rzeczywisty, CRBR, kontrola GIIF albo KAS, a także po ludzku "czy moje biuro podlega pod AML", "co muszę mieć na kontrolę", "klient nie chce powiedzieć, kto naprawdę jest właścicielem". NIE używaj… ([DariuszTrachimowicz/katalog-skilli](https://github.com/DariuszTrachimowicz/katalog-skilli/tree/HEAD/skille/procedura-aml))
- **procedural**: Store and retrieve reusable step-by-step procedures with versioning, applicability checks, and verification before execution. ([SamoTech/skills-tree](https://github.com/SamoTech/skills-tree/tree/HEAD/agent-skills/procedural) · ★4)
- **procedural**: Curate procedural-memory candidates (the memory stack Layer 6) — the conflicts, merges and batch distillation that auto-promotion leaves to a human. A rules mode distils SKILLS into CLAUDE.md rule candidates. Triggers on - "/procedural", "review the recipes", "procedural memory", "distil rules". ([blackboostervoid/inkling](https://github.com/blackboostervoid/inkling/tree/HEAD/skills/procedural))
- **Procedural Animation Master**: Comprehensive skill for procedural animation in 2D games. Covers spring-damper systems, inverse kinematics, follow-the-leader chains, phase offsets, spline dynamics, ground adaptation, anticipation, procedural posing, two-skeleton architecture, and emergent personality. Based on analysis of Rain World, Binding of Isaac, Enter the Gungeon, and production codebase in pygame. ([Franko12345/Roguelizard](https://github.com/Franko12345/Roguelizard/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/procedural-animation-master) · ★2)
- **Procedural chunk world**: Use this when standing up or extending a browser cartoon open-world ground plane with seeded chunks, streaming, and distance LOD. ([Bannon-github/skill-library](https://github.com/Bannon-github/skill-library/tree/HEAD/bot-workflows/procedural-chunk-world))
- **Procedural Dungeon & Level Generation Architect**: Algorithms for procedural map generation: Binary Space Partitioning (BSP), Cellular Automata, Delaunay Triangulation + MST, and Drunkard Walk. ([FrancisCuarto/OrbitKit](https://github.com/FrancisCuarto/OrbitKit/tree/HEAD/skills/procgen-dungeon-architect))
